	private void RefreshItems(Item[] array, bool onlyIfVariantChanged)
	{

	}

			}
		}
	}

	public void Serialize(BinaryWriter writer, ItemSerializationContext context)
	{
		if (context == ItemSerializationContext.SavingAndLoading)
		{
			writer.Write(type);
			writer.Write(stack);
			writer.Write(prefix);
		}
	}

	public void DeserializeFrom(BinaryReader reader, ItemSerializationContext context)
	{
		if (context == ItemSerializationContext.SavingAndLoading)
		{
			netDefaults(reader.ReadInt32());
			stack = reader.ReadInt32();
			Prefix(reader.ReadByte());
		}
		if (type >= ItemID.Count)
		{
			TurnToAir();
		}
	}

	public void ResetPrefix()
	{
		if (prefix != 0)
		{
			prefix = 0;
			Refresh(onlyIfVariantChanged: false);
		}
	}

	public void Refresh(bool onlyIfVariantChanged = true)
	{
		if (!IsAir && (!onlyIfVariantChanged || ItemVariants.SelectVariant(type) != Variant))
		{
			bool flag = favorited;
			int num = stack;
			int num2 = type;
			int prefixWeWant = prefix;
			SetDefaults(num2);
			Prefix(prefixWeWant);
			stack = num;
			favorited = flag;
		}
	}
}

```
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Terraria.GameContent;

public class ItemShopSellbackHelper
{
	private class ItemMemo
	{
		public readonly int type;

		public readonly int prefix;

		public int stack;

		public ItemMemo(Item item)
		{
			type = item.type;
			prefix = item.prefix;
			stack = item.stack;
		}

		public bool Matches(Item item)
		{
			if (item.IsConsideredSameItemAsType(type))
			{
				return item.prefix == prefix;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	private List<ItemMemo> _memos = new List<ItemMemo>();

	public void Add(Item item)
	{
		ItemMemo itemMemo = _memos.Find((ItemMemo x) => x.Matches(item));
		if (itemMemo != null)
		{
			itemMemo.stack += item.stack;
		}
		else
		{
			_memos.Add(new ItemMemo(item));
		}
	}

	public void Clear()
	{
		_memos.Clear();
	}

	public int GetAmount(Item item)
	{
		return _memos.Find((ItemMemo x) => x.Matches(item))?.stack ?? 0;
	}

	public int Remove(Item item)
	{
		ItemMemo itemMemo = _memos.Find((ItemMemo x) => x.Matches(item));
		if (itemMemo == null)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		int stack = itemMemo.stack;
		itemMemo.stack -= item.stack;
		if (itemMemo.stack <= 0)
		{
			_memos.Remove(itemMemo);
			return stack;
		}
		return stack - itemMemo.stack;
	}
}

```

  private shopHappinessMul = 1;

  /** 对话 NPC 的快乐度快照（Player.currentShoppingSettings，SetTalkNPC 时计算；
   *  价格乘子 + 心情报告行，商店/购买/心情浮层共用） */
  private dialogHappiness: { priceMul: number; report: HappinessReportLine[] } | null = null;

  /** 心情报告行 → l10n 文案（ShopHelper.AddHappinessReportText :194-203 的渲染侧）：
   *  {BiomeName} ← TownNPCMoodBiomes.<nameKey>，{NPCName} ← NPC.GetFullnameByID（Lang.npcName） */
  private happinessLineText(l: HappinessReportLine): string {
    let s = Lang.text(l.key);
    if (l.biomeNameKey !== undefined) {
      s = s.replace('{BiomeName}', Lang.text(`TownNPCMoodBiomes.${l.biomeNameKey}`));
    }
    if (l.npcId !== undefined) {
      s = s.replace('{NPCName}', Lang.npcName(l.npcId) ?? '');
    }
    return s;
  }

  /** 「快乐度」按钮（ReportHappiness.Interact，NPCInteractions.cs:289-313）：
   *  打开详情浮层——报告逐条 + 价格倍率（原版把 HappinessReport 写进 npcChatText） */
  private openNpcHappinessPanel(): void {
    const npc = this.dialogNpc;
    if (!npc || !this.dialogHappiness || this.dialogHappiness.report.length === 0) return;
    const typeName = Lang.npcName(npc.vanillaId) ?? npc.npcKey;
    const name = npc.givenName ? Lang.text('Game.NPCTitle', npc.givenName, typeName) : typeName;
    this.cb.onNpcHappiness?.({
      name,
      lines: this.dialogHappiness.report.map((l) => this.happinessLineText(l)),
      priceMul: this.dialogHappiness.priceMul,
    });
  }

  /** 商店购买:扣币 + 入包 + 刷新商店面板(可购态/余额) */
  npcShopBuy(vanillaId: number): void {
    const list = this.dialogNpc ? this.shopStockFor(this.dialogNpc) : [];
    const entry = list.find(([id]) => id === vanillaId);
    if (!entry) return;
    // 折扣卡（discountAvailable：PriceAdjustment 0.8，Main.cs:41028 同源）× 快乐度乘子
    const price = Math.max(1, Math.round(entry[1] * (this.player.equipStats.discount ? 0.8 : 1) * this.shopHappinessMul));
    if (this.coinsOwned() < price || !this.spendCopper(price)) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NotEnoughCoins'));
      this.openNpcShop();
      return;
    }
    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vanillaId];
    if (key && ITEM_BY_KEY[key] !== undefined) this.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[key], 1);
    this.sfx.play('coin');
    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    this.openNpcShop();
  }

  /** 骷髅商离屏计时（CheckActive timeLeft=750 语义） */

  /** 旅行商人到访/离场轮（每 tick）。顺带：骷髅商离屏 timeLeft 消失（CheckActive 语义） */
  private updateTravellingMerchant(): void {
    const w = this.world;
    const t = w.clock.timeOfDay;
    // 骷髅商：离屏累计 750 tick 消失（在屏重置——CheckActive 屏内矩形语义；
    // 入侵不影响其消散）
    const sm = this.entities.npcs.find(
      (n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && n.npcKey === 'skeleton_merchant') as TownNPC | undefined;
    if (sm) {
      const ds = Math.max(Math.abs(sm.cx - this.player.cx), Math.abs(sm.cy - this.player.cy));
      if (ds > TILE * 62) {
        if (++this.skeletonMerchantDespawnT >= 750) { sm.dead = true; this.skeletonMerchantDespawnT = 0; }
      } else this.skeletonMerchantDespawnT = 0;
    }
    // 入侵/月事件进行中旅行商不到访（SpawnTravelNPC :5189 门；月事件原版经 !dayTime
    // 间接压制——此处同入驻轮显式补）
    if (invasionActive(w) || moonEventActive(w)) return;
    const tm = this.entities.npcs.find(
      (n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && n.npcKey === 'travelling_merchant') as TownNPC | undefined;
    if (tm) {
      // 离场：白天过 48600 或入夜，且玩家离屏后移除（UnspawnTravelNPC）。
      // 48600/54000 = 0.9（昼内进度，Main.cs:64477——曾误按 0.8 = 43200 提前一小时赶人）
      if (!w.clock.isDay || (t - Clock.DAWN) / 0.625 > 0.9) {
        const d = Math.hypot(tm.cx - this.player.cx, tm.cy - this.player.cy);
        if (d > TILE * 62) {
          tm.dead = true;
          this.newText(Lang.text('LegacyMisc.35', Lang.npcName(368) ?? ''), 50, 125, 255);
        }
      }

    }

      if (tn.vanillaId === 37 || tn.vanillaId === 368 || tn.vanillaId === 453) continue;

      const d = Math.hypot(tnAnchor.x - anchor.x, tnAnchor.y - anchor.y);

      forest: !!(sc && !sc.zoneCorrupt && !sc.zoneCrimson && !sc.zoneHallow && !sc.zoneSnow
        && !sc.zoneJungle && !sc.zoneDesert && !sc.zoneGlowshroom && !sc.zoneBeach
        && !sc.zoneDungeon && !sc.zoneDirtLayerHeight && !sc.zoneRockLayerHeight),
      ocean: !!sc?.zoneBeach, snow: !!sc?.zoneSnow, desert: !!sc?.zoneDesert,
      jungle: !!sc?.zoneJungle, underground: !!sc?.zoneDirtLayerHeight || !!sc?.zoneRockLayerHeight,
      hallow: !!sc?.zoneHallow, mushroom: !!sc?.zoneGlowshroom,
===== shopStockFor
  private shopStockFor(npc: TownNPC): Array<[number, number]> {
    if (npc.npcKey === 'travelling_merchant') return this.travelStock.slice();  // 动态股票
    const stock = Game.SHOPSTOCK[String(npc.vanillaId)];
    if (!stock) return [];
    const out: Array<[number, number]> = stock.filter((s) => this.shopCondOk(s.cond)).map((s) => [s.id, s.price ?? 100] as [number, number]);
    // 派对女孩灯笼夜专柜（Chest.cs:2282：LanternNight.LanternsUp → 上架 4702 放飞灯笼；
    // extract-shops.mjs 未捕获该 if 门——与 moonPhase 同类缺口，此处直接补）。
    // 原版 SetDefaults(4702) 无价格覆盖 → 按 item value 100 铜出售
    if (npc.vanillaId === 208 && LanternNight.lanternsUp) out.push([4702, 100] as [number, number]);
    // 油漆师基础 12 色（Chest.cs:2424-2428 `for (int j = 1073; j <= 1084; j++)` 循环上架——
    // extract-shops.mjs 不展开 for 循环，vanilla-shopstock.json 缺 1073-1084，
    // 与 4702/moonPhase 同类提取缺口，此处按原版循环直补；价格 25 = Item.value（Item.cs:13374））
    if (npc.vanillaId === 227) {
      for (let j = 1073; j <= 1084; j++) out.push([j, 25] as [number, number]);
    }
    // ---- 月相分档专柜（extract-shops.mjs 只记 cond:'moonPhase' 未捕获 case 值——
    //      剔除恒真条目，按 Chest.cs switch 实表补 8 相位档）----
    const phase = this.world.clock.moonPhase;
    const price = (id: number) => ITEM_VALUES[String(id)] ?? 100;
    const day = this.world.clock.isDay;
    const add = (ids: number[]) => { for (const id of ids) out.push([id, price(id)] as [number, number]); };
    if (npc.npcKey !== 'travelling_merchant') {
      // 上述条目按相位重排（JSON 无 moonPhase 条目的 NPC 也进——分档表是权威）
      switch (npc.vanillaId) {
        case 453: { // 骷髅商（Chest.cs:2780-2940）
          add([[284], [946], [3069], [4341], [285], [953], [3068], [3084]][phase]);
          add(phase % 2 === 0 ? [3001] : this.world.flags.hardMode ? [188] : [28]);
          add(!day || phase === 0 ? [3002] : [282]);
          add(phase === 0 || phase === 1 || phase === 4 || phase === 5 ? [3003] : [40]);
          add([[3310], [3313], [3312], [3311]][phase % 4]);
          add(phase === 1 || phase === 2 ? [5640] : phase === 3 || phase === 5 ? [5641] : [5642]);
          if (phase === 0 && !day) add([3043]); // 满月夜魔法灯笼（:2930-2934）
          break;
        }
        case 20: { // 树妖月相轮换货（Chest.cs:1650-1697；NPCInteractions.cs:491
          // `Shop(20, 3)` 槽位 3=树妖(20)——提取器把相位 switch 摊平成 hardMode/
          // !hardMode 条目，先清 out 里的 4430-4441 再按相位补；
          // 逐相实表提纯到 townNpcProfiles.dryadMoonPhaseStock）
          for (let i = out.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (out[i][0] >= 4430 && out[i][0] <= 4441) out.splice(i, 1);
          }
          add(dryadMoonPhaseStock(phase, !!this.world.flags.hardMode));
          break;
        }
        case 54: // 裁缝套装（:1795-1923）
          if (phase === 0) { add([245, 246]); if (!day) add([1288, 1289]); }
          else if (phase === 1) add([325, 326]);
          else if (this.world.flags.hardMode) {
            if (phase === 2) add([869]);
            else if (phase === 3) add([4994, 4997]);
            else if (phase === 4) add([864, 865]);
            else if (phase === 5) add([4995, 4998]);
            else if (phase === 6) add([873, 874, 875]);
            else add([4996, 4999]);
          }
          break;
        case 124: // 机械师（:2058-2062：渔夫在场且奇相位 → 机械师钓竿）
          if (phase % 2 === 1 && this.entities.npcs.some((n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 369)) {
            add([2295]);
          }

        case 353: if ((phase % 2 === 0) === day) add([1981]); break; // 造型师时光染料（:2720-2724）

  /** 铜币入包（按面额拆分含铂金档；spendCopper 的镜像；vi_71-74 单轨） */
  private gainCopper(n: number): void {
    const inv = this.player.inv;
    const plat = Math.floor(n / 1000000);
    const gold = Math.floor((n % 1000000) / 10000);
    const silver = Math.floor((n % 10000) / 100);
    const copper = n % 100;
    if (plat) inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.COIN.PLATINUM], plat);
    if (gold) inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.COIN.GOLD], gold);
    if (silver) inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.COIN.SILVER], silver);
    if (copper) inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.COIN.COPPER], copper);
  }

  /** 护士治疗:费用 = 缺失生命 × 0.75 铜(下限 3);满血拒绝 */
  /** Main.GetNurseHealCost（Main.cs:39463-39511）1:1：(缺血 + 100×可移除 debuff)
   *  × 进度倍率链（石头人200/世花150/三王100/困难60/骷髅王|蜂后25/世吞|克脑10/
   *  EoC 3）× 专家 2 ×（折扣 0.8 未实装——discountAvailable 恒 false，登记） */
  private nurseHealCost(): number {
    const p = this.player;
    const f = this.world.flags;
    let num = p.maxHp - p.hp;
    // 可移除 debuff：buffTime>60 且非 NurseCannotRemoveDebuff 表——本仓 debuff 族
    // 全部可移除（该排除表多为 boss 专属 debuff，未实装，登记）
    num += 100 * p.buffs.debuffCount(60);
    if (f['downed_245']) num *= 200;        // Golem
    else if (f['downed_262']) num *= 150;   // Plantera
    else if (f['downed_125'] || f['downed_126'] || f['downed_127'] || f['downed_134']) num *= 100;
    else if (f.hardMode) num *= 60;
    else if (f.downedSkeletron || f['downed_222']) num *= 25;   // Boss3 | QueenBee
    else if (f['downed_13'] || f['downed_14'] || f['downed_15']) num *= 10;  // EoW/BoC
    else if (f['downed_4']) num *= 3;       // EoC
    if (this.world.isExpert) num *= 2;
    return Math.floor(num);
  }

  private nurseHeal() {
    const p = this.player;
    if (p.hp >= p.maxHp && p.buffs.debuffCount(60) === 0) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NurseHealthy'));
      return;
    }
    const cost = this.nurseHealCost();
    if (cost <= 0 || this.coinsOwned() < cost || !this.spendCopper(cost)) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NotEnoughCoins'));
      return;
    }
    p.hp = p.maxHp;
    p.buffs.clearDebuffs();   // 护士同时清 debuff（NPCChatText_DoNurseHeal 语义）
    this.sfx.play('drink');
    this.cb.onBuffsChanged?.();
    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NurseHealed'));
    // 成就：护士付费累计（AchievementsHelper.HandleNurseService → FREQUENT_FLYER
    // 10000 铜；NurseChat 治疗结算点）
    this.achievements.handleNurseService(cost);
  }

  /** 扣铜币:全背包四档币换算清点,整额清空后按面额找零(vanilla BuyItem 语义;vi_71-74 单轨) */
  private spendCopper(n: number): boolean {
    const inv = this.player.inv;
    if (this.coinsOwned() < n) return false;
    const change = this.coinsOwned() - n;
    for (let i = 0; i < inv.slots.length; i++) {
      const sl = inv.slots[i];
      if (sl && coinIndexOf(sl.id) >= 0) inv.slots[i] = null;
    }
    // 按面额回流(铂→金→银→铜,栈内合并)
    const plat = Math.floor(change / 1000000);
    const gold = Math.floor((change % 1000000) / 10000);
    const silver = Math.floor((change % 10000) / 100);
    const copper = change % 100;
    if (plat) inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.COIN.PLATINUM], plat);
    if (gold) inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.COIN.GOLD], gold);
    if (silver) inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.COIN.SILVER], silver);
    if (copper) inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.COIN.COPPER], copper);
    return true;
  }

  // （旧 isValidHouse 启发式已删除——房屋判定移至 src/world/Housing.ts，
  //  泛洪填充 + 原版 wallHouse 墙表 + 桌椅灯需求，见 checkRoom/findFreeHouse）

  // ================= GameHooks 实现 =================

  /** 掉落系统补充上下文（灵魂/钥匙/风筝等条件）：玩家 zone + 季节 + 血月 + 风 */
  dropContext(): DropExtras {
    const s = this.scene;
    const c = this.world.clock;
    return {
      zoneHallow: s.zoneHallow, zoneCorrupt: s.zoneCorrupt, zoneCrimson: s.zoneCrimson,
      zoneJungle: s.zoneJungle, zoneSnow: s.zoneSnow, zoneDesert: s.zoneDesert,
      zoneDungeon: s.zoneDungeon, zoneBeach: s.zoneBeach,
===== coinsOwned
    this.entities.add(new Tombstone(p.cx, p.cy, this.coinsOwned(), deathText, p.facing), 'props');
  }

  /** 身家（铜币计价）——商店/重铸扣费与墓碑金币门槛（>100000 → 金墓碑 527-531）。
   *  vi_71-74 单轨（本地 coin_* 双轨段已随退役移除） */
  coinsOwned(): number {
    const p = this.player;
    let total = 0;
    for (const s of p.inv.slots) {
      if (!s) continue;
      const ci = coinIndexOf(s.id);
      if (ci >= 0) total += s.stack * COIN_VALUES[ci];
    }
    return total;
  }

  /** 角色名（死亡文本/墓碑碑文用；未命名回退） */
  playerName(): string {
    return this.player.appearance?.name?.trim() || Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.DefaultPlayerName');
  }

  /** 旗 buff：该 npc 是否被屏内旗覆盖（ItemID.Sets.BannerStrength 普通族） */
  bannerNpcActive(npcId: number): boolean { return this._bannerNpcs.has(npcId); }

  respawnPlayer() {
    const p = this.player;
    // 硬核角色已消亡（Player.cs:16894 difficulty==2 死亡后 ghost=true 不再重生；
    // 本仓最小集 = hardcoreDead 标记 → 不允许重生，死亡画面停留）
    if (p.appearance?.hardcoreDead) return;

    const wasPvpDeath = p.lastPvpDeath;
    p.lastPvpDeath = false;
    p.hp = p.maxHp;
    p.dead = false;
    p.iframes = wasPvpDeath ? 300 : 180;
    p.respawnTimer = 0;
    p.immuneAlpha = 0;
    this.lostCoins = 0;
    p.x = this.world.spawnX * TILE + 8;
    p.y = (this.world.spawnY - 3) * TILE;
    p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0;
    // 呼吸/岩浆宽限重置（Player.cs:37158 lavaTime=lavaMax / :37173 breath=breathMax；
    // 旧实现两者都没重置——戴护身符重生后进岩浆会被误判宽限已耗尽）

86	export function ammoSlotAccepts(id: number): boolean {
87	  const d = ITEM_DEFS[id];
88	  if (!d) return false;
89	  const vid = d.vid ?? viIdFromKey(d.key);
90	  if (vid < 0) return d.ammoId !== undefined && d.ammoId > 0; // legacy 注册表自带 ammoId
91	  if (vid === 353) return true;
92	  const f = itemFuncOfVid(vid);
93	  if (f?.notAmmo) return false;
94	  if (vid === 530 || vid === 849 || PAINT_OR_COATING.has(vid)) return true;
95	  if ((f?.bait ?? 0) > 0) return true;
96	  return (itemCombat(vid)?.ammo ?? 0) > 0;
97	}

120	  miscEquips: Array<InvItem | null>;

122	  miscDyes: Array<InvItem | null>;
123	  /** 三套 Loadout（EquipmentLoadout = armor[20]+dye[10]，Swap :65-82 元素级互换） */
124	  loadouts: Array<{ armor: Array<InvItem | null>; dye: Array<InvItem | null> }>;
125	  /** 当前 Loadout 序号（0-2） */
126	  currentLoadout = 0;
127	  /** 恶魔之心 3335(Player.cs:44575-44583)解锁第六配饰槽(armor[8];基座 5=3-7) */
128	  extraAccessory = false;
129	  /** 功能配饰槽上界(不含):基座 5,恶魔之心 6(UpdateEquipAccessories 语义) */
130	  get accSlotMax(): number { return ACC_ARMOR_START + 5 + (this.extraAccessory ? 1 : 0); }
131	  /** 垃圾桶持久槽（Player.trashItem :1380——关背包保留、死亡清空 :53394） */
132	  trash: InvItem | null = null;
133	  selected = 0; // 快捷栏选中格
134	
135	  constructor() {
136	    this.slots = Array(INV_SIZE).fill(null);
137	    this.armor = Array(ARMOR_TOTAL).fill(null);
138	    this.dye = Array(DYE_SLOTS).fill(null);
139	    this.miscEquips = Array(MISC_SLOTS).fill(null);
140	    this.miscDyes = Array(MISC_SLOTS).fill(null);
141	    this.loadouts = Array.from({ length: LOADOUT_COUNT }, () => ({
142	      armor: Array(ARMOR_TOTAL).fill(null),
143	      dye: Array(DYE_SLOTS).fill(null),
144	    }));
145	  }
146	
147	  /** 切换 Loadout（Player.TrySwitchingLoadout :5430-5434：先 Swap 当前套装备份仓、
148	   *  再 Swap 目标仓——玩家身上即换上目标套装，原套装入当前备份仓） */
149	  trySwitchingLoadout(idx: number): void {
150	    if (idx === this.currentLoadout || idx < 0 || idx >= LOADOUT_COUNT) return;
151	    const swap = (a: Array<InvItem | null>, b: Array<InvItem | null>, n: number) => {
152	      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const t = a[i]; a[i] = b[i]; b[i] = t; }
153	    };
154	    const cur = this.loadouts[this.currentLoadout];
155	    const next = this.loadouts[idx];
156	    swap(this.armor, cur.armor, ARMOR_TOTAL);
157	    swap(this.dye, cur.dye, DYE_SLOTS);
158	    swap(this.armor, next.armor, ARMOR_TOTAL);
159	    swap(this.dye, next.dye, DYE_SLOTS);
160	    this.currentLoadout = idx;
161	  }
162

169	  private isPaintSlot(i: number): boolean {

172	  }
173

176	  findPaintSlot(): number {
177	    for (let i = AMMO_START; i < AMMO_START + AMMO_SLOTS; i++) {
178	      if (this.isPaintSlot(i)) return i;

180	    for (let i = 0; i < INV_SIZE; i++) {

188	  heldPaintLookup(): number {

190	    return i < 0 ? 0 : (ITEM_DEFS[this.slots[i]!.id]?.paint ?? 0);
191	  }
192	
193	  heldDef() {
194	    const it = this.heldItem();
195	    return it ? ITEM_DEFS[it.id] : null;
196	  }
197	
198	  /** 已装备的配饰列表（armor[3-9]，原版 Player.cs:36326 循环范围） */
199	  equippedAccessories(): number[] {
200	    const out: number[] = [];

202	      const s = this.armor[i];
203	      if (s && ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.accessory) out.push(s.id);

207

  /** 原版消息列(Main.NewText 入口) */
  private chatMonitor: ChatMonitor | null = null;
  /** 消息缓冲:ChatMonitor 未建(initInGame 之前,如新世界 afterWorldLoad
   *  发出的 NPC 入驻公告)时暂存,initInGame 创建后回放——曾直接丢弃 */
  private chatPending: Array<[string, number, number, number, number]> = [];
  chatMessage(text: string, r = 255, g = 255, b = 255, width = -1): void {
    if (this.chatMonitor) this.chatMonitor.message(text, r, g, b, width);
    else this.chatPending.push([text, r, g, b, width]);
  }

  // ---- NPC 对话系统(SetTalkNPC + GetChat;内容在 Game 生成,此处纯渲染) ----
  private npcDialog: NpcDialog | null = null;
  private npcShop: NpcShop | null = null;

  showNpcDialog(name: string, chat: string, buttons: Array<{ id: NpcButtonId; label: string }>, portrait?: string | null): void {
    if (!this.npcDialog) {
      this.npcDialog = new NpcDialog(this.root, (id) => {
        this.npcShop?.close();
        this.npcShop = null;
        this.game?.npcDialogButton(id);
      });
    }
    this.npcDialog.open(name, chat, buttons, portrait);
  }

  closeNpcDialog(): void {
    this.npcDialog?.close();
    this.npcShop?.close();
    this.npcShop = null;
    this.npcHappiness?.close();   // 心情浮层随对话框一并关闭
  }

  showNpcShop(title: string, items: Array<{ key: string; vanillaId: number; name: string; price: number }>, copper: number, happinessPct?: number): void {
    if (!this.npcShop) {
      this.npcShop = new NpcShop(this.root,
        (vid) => this.game?.npcShopBuy(vid),
        () => { this.npcShop?.close(); this.npcShop = null; });
    }
    // 图标由 UI 侧按原版 id 补(atlas 管线)
    const entries: ShopEntry[] = items.map((it) => ({
      ...it,
      iconUrl: this.game ? iconUrl(this.game, it.vanillaId) || '' : '',
    }));
    this.npcShop.open(title, entries, copper, happinessPct);
    // 懒加载晚到升级(2026-08-13 枚举 review):开店瞬间 iconUrl miss(空串/程序化
    // 兜底)此前会冻结到关店——refreshAll 不触 npcShop。有界轮询就绪后原地补图,
    // 关店/超时即停(iconCache 只缓存命中,iconUrl 天然可升级)
    const g = this.game;
    if (g) {
      const initial = new Map(entries.map((e) => [e.vanillaId, e.iconUrl]));
      let tries = 0;
      const timer = setInterval(() => {
        const shop = this.npcShop;
        if (!shop || !shop.isOpen || ++tries > 15) { clearInterval(timer); return; }
        for (const e of entries) {
          const url = iconUrl(g, e.vanillaId);
          if (url && url !== initial.get(e.vanillaId)) {
            initial.set(e.vanillaId, url);
            shop.setRowIcon(e.vanillaId, url);
          }
        }
      }, 600);
    }
  }

  /** NPC 快乐度详情浮层（ReportHappiness.Interact） */
  private npcHappiness: NpcHappinessPanel | null = null;

  showNpcHappiness(info: HappinessInfo): void {
    this.npcHappiness?.close();
    this.npcHappiness = new NpcHappinessPanel(this.root, () => { this.npcHappiness = null; });
    this.npcHappiness.open(info);
  }

  closeNpcHappiness(): void {
    this.npcHappiness?.close();
  }

122	/** 铜币计价格式化(原版 tooltip:金/银/铜) */
123	export function formatCopper(v: number): string {
124	  const gold = Math.floor(v / 10000);
125	  const silver = Math.floor((v % 10000) / 100);
126	  const copper = v % 100;
127	  let s = '';
128	  if (gold) s += `${gold}金`;
129	  if (silver) s += `${silver}银`;
130	  if (copper || !s) s += `${copper}铜`;
131	  return s;
132	}

134	export class NpcDialog {
135	  private panel: HTMLElement | null = null;
136	  private escHandler: ((e: KeyboardEvent) => void) | null = null;
137	
138	  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private onButton: (id: NpcButtonId) => void) {
139	    if (!document.getElementById('sw-npc-style')) {
140	      const style = document.createElement('style');
141	      style.id = 'sw-npc-style';
142	      style.textContent = CSS;
143	      document.head.appendChild(style);
144	    }
145	  }
146	
147	  /** 打开对话框:name=NPC 名,chat=台词,buttons=选项(原版 Lang.inter 标签) */
148	  open(name: string, chat: string, buttons: Array<{ id: NpcButtonId; label: string }>, portrait?: string | null) {
149	    this.close();
150	    const panel = document.createElement('div');
151	    panel.className = 'sw-npc-dialog';
152	    const btns = buttons.map((b) => `<button data-b="${b.id}">${b.label}</button>`).join('');
153	    // 头像(原版 Main.npcChatPortrait = Images/TownNPCs/Portraits/Portrait_<Name>，SetTalkNPC :4196;
154	    // 打开时 DoNPCPortraitHop 弹跳)——无头像类型(如骷髅商人外的野兽)原版也不显示
155	    const portraitHtml = portrait
156	      ? `<img class="sw-npc-portrait" src="${portrait}">`
157	      : '';
158	    panel.innerHTML = `<div class="sw-npc-body">${portraitHtml}<div class="sw-npc-main">`
159	      + `<div class="sw-npc-name">${name}</div><div class="sw-npc-chat">${chat}</div>`
160	      + `<div class="sw-npc-btns">${btns}</div></div></div>`;
161	    panel.querySelectorAll('button').forEach((el) => {
162	      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
163	        UISfx.click(); // 原版对话按钮 MenuTick(id 12)
164	        this.onButton((el as HTMLElement).dataset.b as NpcButtonId);
165	      });
166	    });
167	    this.parent.appendChild(panel);
168	    this.panel = panel;
169	    this.escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.code === 'Escape') this.onButton('close'); };
170	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
171	  }
172	
173	  close() {
174	    if (this.escHandler) window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler);
175	    this.escHandler = null;
176	    this.panel?.remove();
177	    this.panel = null;
178	  }
179	
180	  get isOpen(): boolean { return this.panel !== null; }
181	}

  private shiftClick(i: number, where: SlotWhere): void {
    const inv = this.game.player.inv;
    if (where === 'armor' || where === 'dye' || where === 'misc' || where === 'miscDye') {
      // Unequip（:634-649）：卸回背包
      const arr = this.equipArray(where);
      const cur = arr[i];
      if (!cur || cur.favorited) return;
      const left = inv.add(cur.id, cur.stack);
      if (left === 0) arr[i] = null;
      this.refreshAll();
      return;
    }
    if (where === 'inv') {
      const cur = inv.slots[i];
      if (!cur || cur.favorited) return; // 收藏禁转移（:566）
      if (this.openChest) {
        // TryPlacingInChest（QuickStack 语义：先合并箱内已有类型再空格）
        const chest = this.openChest;
        let moved = 0;
        for (let k = 0; k < chest.items.length; k++) {
          const cs = chest.items[k];
          if (cs && cs.id === cur.id && cs.stack < ITEM_DEFS[cur.id].maxStack) {
            const put = Math.min(ITEM_DEFS[cur.id].maxStack - cs.stack, cur.stack);
            cs.stack += put; cur.stack -= put; moved += put;
            if (cur.stack <= 0) break;
          }
        }
        for (let k = 0; k < chest.items.length && cur.stack > 0; k++) {
          if (!chest.items[k]) { chest.items[k] = cur; inv.slots[i] = null; return; }
        }
        if (cur.stack <= 0) inv.slots[i] = null;
        if (moved > 0 || !inv.slots[i]) this.refreshAll();
        return;
      }
      // 无箱→垃圾桶（覆盖旧内容，OverrideLeftClick cursorOverride 6 :689-706）
      inv.trash = cur;
      inv.slots[i] = null;
      this.game.sfx.play('menuTick', 1);
      this.refreshAll();
      return;
    }
    if (where === 'chest') {
      // TransferFromChest（:626-633）：箱→背包
      const chest = this.openChest;
      if (!chest) return;
      const cur = chest.items[i];
      if (!cur) return;
      const left = inv.add(cur.id, cur.stack);
      if (left === 0) chest.items[i] = null;
      else cur.stack = left;
      this.refreshAll();
    }
  }

  // ---- 拖拽系统：按下起点 + 光标持有物（ghost 跟随） ----
  private dragFrom: { i: number; where: SlotWhere; sx: number; sy: number } | null = null;
  private ghostEl: HTMLElement | null = null;
  private mouseX = 0; private mouseY = 0;

  private installDragListeners() {
    // 点击面板外的世界区域：光标持有物直接丢出（原版行为）
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      if (!this.heldStack) return;
      const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      if (t?.closest?.('.sw-slot') || t?.closest?.('.sw-panel') || t?.closest?.('.sw-btn')) return;
      this.throwHeldToWorld();
      this.refreshAll();
    });
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
      this.mouseX = e.clientX; this.mouseY = e.clientY;
      this.moveGhost();
      // 未持有物品时按下并移动 >4px → 整叠拾取开始拖拽
      if (this.dragFrom && !this.heldStack) {
        const d = Math.hypot(e.clientX - this.dragFrom.sx, e.clientY - this.dragFrom.sy);
        if (d > 4) this.pickUp(this.dragFrom.i, this.dragFrom.where);
      }
    });
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
      if (!this.dragFrom) return;
      const from = this.dragFrom;
      this.dragFrom = null;
      if (e.button !== 0) return;
      if (this.heldStack) {
        this.resolveDrop(from);
      } else {
        // 无拖动的普通点击：快捷栏（面板关）选中；否则拾取到光标（点击移动仍可用）
        const panelOpen = this.invPanel && this.invPanel.style.display === 'block';
        if (from.where === 'inv' && from.i < 10 && !panelOpen) {
          this.game.player.inv.selected = from.i;
          this.refreshHotbar();
        } else {
          this.pickUp(from.i, from.where);
        }
      }
      this.refreshAll();
    });
  }

  /** 整叠拾取到光标 */
  private pickUp(i: number, where: SlotWhere) {
    const cur = this.slotContent(where, i);
    if (!cur) return;
    if (this.heldStack && this.heldStack.id !== cur.id) return;
    if (where === 'inv') this.game.player.inv.slots[i] = null;
    else if (where === 'armor') this.game.player.inv.armor[i] = null;
    else if (where === 'dye') this.game.player.inv.dye[i] = null;
    else if (where === 'misc') this.game.player.inv.miscEquips[i] = null;
    else if (where === 'miscDye') this.game.player.inv.miscDyes[i] = null;
    else if (this.openChest) this.openChest.items[i] = null;
    if (this.heldStack) this.heldStack.stack += cur.stack;
    else this.heldStack = { id: cur.id, stack: cur.stack, prefix: cur.prefix, ...(cur.placeStyle !== undefined ? { placeStyle: cur.placeStyle } : {}) }; // 保留词缀+实例样式
    this.refreshAll();
  }

Important detail: sellback uses calcForBuying for the item as computed NOW — meaning the sellback price = buy price at current happiness. And isAShopItem items (shop stock) with discount → calcForSelling = discounted buy price → those sell at 20% off value /5.

  private formatDialogTags(s: string): string {
    const townName = (key: string): string => {
      const inst = this.entities.npcs.find(
        (n) => n instanceof TownNPC && !n.dead && (n as TownNPC).npcKey === key) as TownNPC | undefined;
      const base = Lang.npcName(TOWN_NPC_IDS[key]?.id ?? 0) ?? key;
      return inst?.givenName ? Lang.text('Game.NPCTitle', inst.givenName, base) : base;
    };
    return s
      .replace(/\{PlayerName\}/g, this.player.appearance?.name || '泰拉瑞亚')
      .replace(/\{Guide\}/g, townName('guide'))
      .replace(/\{Nurse\}/g, townName('nurse'))
      .replace(/\{Dryad\}/g, townName('dryad'))
      .replace(/\{Demolitionist\}/g, townName('demolitionist'))
      .replace(/\{ArmsDealer\}/g, townName('arms_dealer'))
      .replace(/\{Merchant\}/g, townName('merchant'))
      .replace(/\{Bartender\}/g, Lang.npcName(550) ?? '酒馆老板') // 酒馆老板未实装,恒用类型名
      .replace(/\{WorldEvilStone\}/g,
        Lang.itemName(this.world.crimson ? 836 : 61)   // ItemID.cs:3211/1661：Crimstone/Ebonstone Block
        ?? (this.world.crimson ? '猩红石' : '黑檀石'));
  }

  /** 对话框按钮(SetTalkNPC 后 UI 回调) */
  npcDialogButton(id: NpcButtonId): void {
    const npc = this.dialogNpc;

}

Formula details (JS):

```ts
export function expectedPrices(storeValue, s: SellSettings): { selling: number; buying: number } {
  const pa = s.priceAdjustment ?? 1;
  let selling = storeValue;             // :34915
  let buying = storeValue;              // :34916
  if (s.discountAvailable) {            // :34917
    if (!s.buyOnce) buying = Math.trunc(f32(f32(buying) * f32(0.8)));  // :34921 (int)cast = 截断
    if (s.isAShopItem) selling = buying; // :34923-34926
  }
  if (s.buyOnce) {
    buying = Math.trunc(roundCS(f32(f32(buying) / f32(pa))));   // :34930 (int)Math.Round
    selling = Math.trunc(roundCS(f32(f32(selling) / f32(pa)))); // :34931
  } else {
    buying = Math.trunc(roundCS(f32(f32(buying) * f32(pa))));   // :34935
    selling = Math.trunc(roundCS(f32(f32(selling) / f32(pa)))); // :34936
  }
  if (s.buyOnce) {
    buying = Math.floor(buying / 5);                             // :34940
    if (storeValue !== 0 && buying < 1) buying = 1;              // :34941-34944
  }
  return { selling, buying };
}
```

Wait: `(float)calcForBuying * 0.8f` — both operands float → float result. f32(buying)*f32(0.8): in JS the multiply is double, but both operands are exactly float32 values; the product rounded to double may differ from float32 rounding of the product. To emulate: f32(f32(buying) * 0.8). Since f32(0.8) = 0.8 double ≈ 0.800000011920929, and C# uses 0.8f exactly that value, so use `f32(f32(buying) * f32(0.8))` → double multiply of two float32 values, then fround to float32. Correct emulation. ✓

Division: `(float)calcForSelling / currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment` — float/float → float. So `f32(f32(selling) / f32(pa))`. ✓ (pa itself is float in C# — our mul comes from float math chains; fround at the end is a reasonable emulation, matching ScaleStats' convention.)

Then `(int)Math.Round(double)` = banker's round then int cast → roundCS gives an integer already; no trunc needed but Math.trunc is harmless. Keep roundCS only (it returns integer-valued number). Fine.

```ts
export function computeSellValue(vid: number, stack: number, settings: SellSettings = {}): SellQuote {
  const storeValue = itemStoreValue(vid);        // GetStoreValue :49492-49500
  const { selling, buying } = expectedPrices(storeValue, settings);
  if (selling <= 0) return { sellable: false, copper: 0, ... };  // SellItem :34719-34722
  if (stack === -1) stack = ... — hmm, our signature takes explicit stack; vanilla default -1 means item.stack. I'll accept stack=0? Let me just document stack = item.stack (caller passes slot stack).
  let perUnit = Math.floor(selling / 5);  // :34732 long div
  if (perUnit < 1) perUnit = 1;           // :34733-34736
  let total = perUnit * stack;            // :34737-34738
  const amount = settings.sellbackAmount ?? 0;
  if (amount > 0) total += (buying - perUnit) * Math.min(amount, stack);  // :34739-34743
  return { sellable: true, copper: total, perUnit, storeValue, selling, buying };
}
```

Note about `(-num2 + calcForBuying) * Math.Min(amount, item.stack)`: `Math.Min(amount, item.stack)` — item.stack is the full stack being sold. Our `stack` param = the stack being sold. ✓

}

}

            Item.cs:49019-49022     （×5 → 表值恒为 5 的倍数）\n//   ItemShopSellbackHelper        ItemShopSellbackHelper.cs:10-64（店内回购记）\n//   Main.UpdateOldNPCShop         Main.cs:18056-18064     （换店清回购记）\n//   ItemSlot.GetSellOrTrash       ItemSlot.cs:185-196     （Shift+左键 Sell/垃圾桶/钱币豁免）\n//   ItemSlot.OverrideLeftClick cursorOverride==10         ItemSlot.cs:780-798（出售结算）\n//   Main.MouseText_DrawItemTooltip_AddShopLines Main.cs:20305-20345（tooltip 同公式）\n//\n// 本模块纯函数化：全部状态走入参（settings），不触 Game/World/存档。\n// 物品 value 表 = vanilla-shopstock.json values（3138 条全量，tools/extract-shops.mjs\n// 提取自 Item.cs SetDefaults 各 case 的 value= 字段；缺条目 = 原版未赋 value = 0，\n// 见 Item.cs:1485-1489 case 9 木板无 value 行 → value 恒 0）。\n// ============================================================================\nimport shopstockJson from '../data/vanilla-shopstock.json';\nimport { f32, roundCS } from './ScaleStats';\n\n/** 物品 value 表（vanilla-shopstock.json 附带；Game.ts:268 ITEM_VALUES 同源，此处\n *  独立导入以保持纯函数无 Game 依赖） */\nconst ITEM_VALUES = (shopstockJson as { values: Record<string, number> }).values;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Item.GetStoreValue（Item.cs:49492-49500）\n//   ```csharp\n//   public int GetStoreValue() {\n//       if (shopCustomPrice.HasValue) return shopCustomPrice.Value;   // :49494-49497\n//       return value;                                                  // :49499\n//   }\n//   ```\n// shopCustomPrice 未实装（本仓无店主自定义价）→ 恒回落 item.value。\n// 缺表条目（如 2 泥土/9 木板/3 手里剑）= 原版 value 0 = 不可卖。\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nexport function itemStoreValue(vid: number): number {\n  return ITEM_VALUES[String(vid)] ?? 0;\n}\n\n/** 出售输入（对应 SellItem 处引用的原版字段） */\nexport interface SellSettings {\n  /** Player.currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment（Player.cs:2780；NotInShop=1f，\n   *  ShoppingSettings.cs:11-14）。Game 侧传 shopHappinessMul（开对话时快照） */\n  priceAdjustment?: number;\n  /** Player.discountAvailable（Player.cs:2502/:25353）——854 折扣卡/3035 贪婪戒指\n   *  （Player.cs:14424/:14446；本仓 equipStats.discount 同源） */\n  discountAvailable?: boolean;\n  /** Main.shopSellbackHelper.GetAmount(item)（Player.cs:34739；本次开店会话内从该店\n   *  购入的同 type+prefix 数量，ItemShopSellbackHelper.cs:45-52） */\n  sellbackAmount?: number;\n  /** Item.isAShopItem（Item.cs:114；Chest.SetupShop 收尾对全部在售条目置位\n   *  Chest.cs:3519-3522）。卖出路径恒 false：买入走 SetDefaults 重置（ItemSlot.cs:1912）\n   *  → 玩家包内物品不是商店条目实例 */\n  isAShopItem?: boolean;\n  /** Item.buyOnce（Item.cs:240；仅 Chest.AddItemToShop 对卖回店的克隆置位\n   *  Chest.cs:660-671）。卖出路径恒 false */\n  buyOnce?: boolean;\n}\n\n/** GetItemExpectedPrice 输出（Player.cs:34907 出参 calcForSelling/calcForBuying） */\nexport interface ExpectedPrices {\n  /** calcForSelling：单件卖价基数（再 ÷5 才是每件铜币） */\n  selling: number;\n  /** calcForBuying：单件买价基数（回购记退款额用） */\n  buying: number;\n  /** GetStoreValue 原值（:34914） */\n  storeValue: number;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Player.GetItemExpectedPrice（Player.cs:34907-34946）逐行对照\n//   shopSpecialCurrency 分支（:34909-34913）走 CustomCurrencyManager——本仓无自定义\n//   币种商店，登记不实装，直接进铜币主链。\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nexport function expectedPrices(storeValue: number, s: SellSettings = {}): ExpectedPrices {\n  const pa = s.priceAdjustment ?? 1;              // NotInShop = 1f（ShoppingSettings.cs:11-14）\n  let selling = storeValue;                        // :34915 calcForSelling = storeValue\n  let buying = storeValue;                         // :34916 calcForBuying  = storeValue\n  if (s.discountAvailable) {                       // :34917\n    if (!s.buyOnce) {\n      // :34921 calcForBuying = (int)((float)calcForBuying * 0.8f)\n      // (int) 强转 = 向零截断；float 乘法以 f32 逐位对齐\n      buying = Math.trunc(f32(f32(buying) * f32(0.8)));\n    }\n    if (s.isAShopItem) selling = buying;           // :34923-34926\n  }\n  if (s.buyOnce) {\n    // :34930-34931 (int)Math.Round((float)x / PriceAdjustment)\n    // Math.Round(double) 默认银行家舍入（.5 → 就近偶数）→ roundCS\n    buying = roundCS(f32(f32(buying) / f32(pa)));\n    selling = roundCS(f32(f32(selling) / f32(pa)));\n  } else {\n    buying = roundCS(f32(f32(buying) * f32(pa)));  // :34935\n    selling = roundCS(f32(f32(selling) / f32(pa))); // :34936 快乐度作用于卖价（÷ 而非 ×）\n  }\n  if (s.buyOnce) {\n    buying = Math.floor(buying / 5);               // :34940 calcForBuying /= 5L（long 除 = 取整）\n    if (storeValue !== 0 && buying < 1) buying = 1; // :34941-34944\n  }\n  return { selling, buying, storeValue };\n}\n\n/** 出售结算（SellItem 价格段 Player.cs:34716-34743 的纯函数投影） */\nexport interface SellQuote {\n  /** SellItem :34719-34722 calcForSelling<=0 → return false（不可卖） */\n  sellable: boolean;\n  /** 商店应付总额（铜币；SellItem :34738 num = perUnit*stack + 回购差额 :34742） */\n  copper: number;\n  /** 每件铜币（:34732-34736 ÷5 取整 + 最小 1 兜底） */\n  perUnit: number;\n  /** GetItemExpectedPrice 双价（调试/断言用） */\n  prices: ExpectedPrices;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Player.SellItem（Player.cs:34716-34878）价格段 1:1：\n//   :34718  GetItemExpectedPrice(item, out calcForSelling, out calcForBuying)\n//   :34719  if (calcForSelling <= 0) return false;          ← 不可卖清单（value=0 族）\n//   :34732  long num = calcForSelling / 5;                  ← 卖价 = 价值/5\n//   :34733  if (num < 1) num = 1L;                          ← 每件最小 1 铜\n//   :34737  long num2 = num;\n//   :34738  num *= stack;                                   ← 堆叠按 stack 相加\n//   :34739  amount = Main.shopSellbackHelper.GetAmount(item);\n//   :34740  if (amount > 0)\n//   :34742      num += (-num2 + calcForBuying) * Math.Min(amount, item.stack);\n//           ← 店内刚买的按【买价】原额退（差额补齐），仅覆盖记账数量\n//   :34745-34868 币面额换装入包（74 铂/73 金/72 银/71 铜）→ Game.gainCopper 消费\n//   :34869-34876 装不下 → 全量回滚 → Game.npcShopSell 消费\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nexport function computeSellValue(vid: number, stack: number, settings: SellSettings = {}): SellQuote {\n  const prices = expectedPrices(itemStoreValue(vid), settings);\n  if (prices.selling <= 0) {                       // :34719-34722\n    return { sellable: false, copper: 0, perUnit: 0, prices };\n  }\n  let perUnit = Math.floor(prices.selling / 5);    // :34732 long 除 = 向下取整（非负）\n  if (perUnit < 1) perUnit = 1;                    // :34733-34736\n  let total = perUnit * stack;                     // :34737-34738\n  const amount = settings.sellbackAmount ?? 0;     // :34739\n  if (amount > 0) {                                // :34740\n    total += (prices.buying - perUnit) * Math.min(amount, stack); // :34742\n  }\n  return { sellable: true, copper: total, perUnit, prices };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// ItemSlot.GetSellOrTrash（ItemSlot.cs:185-196）——Shift+左键动作选路：\n//   :187  if (Main.npcShop <= 0) return Trash;      ← 店外 = 垃圾桶\n//   :191  if (item.type >= 71 && item.type <= 74) return null;  ← 钱币不卖（不动作）\n//   :195  return Sell;                              ← 店内非钱币 = 卖出\n// Game/UI 的门禁消费此函数（钱币不进卖出，也不回落垃圾桶）。\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nexport function canSellFromShopUi(vid: number): boolean {\n  return !(vid >= 71 && vid <= 74);\n}\n"
}
````

  npcShopBuy(vanillaId: number): void {
    const list = this.dialogNpc ? this.shopStockFor(this.dialogNpc) : [];
    const entry = list.find(([id]) => id === vanillaId);
    if (!entry) return;
    // 折扣卡（discountAvailable：PriceAdjustment 0.8，Main.cs:41028 同源）× 快乐度乘子
    const price = Math.max(1, Math.round(entry[1] * (this.player.equipStats.discount ? 0.8 : 1) * this.shopHappinessMul));
    if (this.coinsOwned() < price || !this.spendCopper(price)) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.NPC.NotEnoughCoins'));
      this.openNpcShop();
      return;
    }
    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vanillaId];
    if (key && ITEM_BY_KEY[key] !== undefined) this.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[key], 1);
    this.sfx.play('coin');
    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    this.openNpcShop();
  }

0) + 1);\n    this.sfx.play('coin');\n    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n    this.openNpcShop();\n  }\n\n  /** 关闭商店面板（UI 回调；原版 Main.npcShop=-1）。换店/关店清回购记\n   *  （Main.UpdateOldNPCShop Main.cs:18056-18064：npcShop != oldNPCShop → Clear） */\n  npcShopClose(): void {\n    this.npcShopOpen = false;\n    this.shopSellbackMemo.clear();\n  }\n\n  /** 商店出售（Player.SellItem Player.cs:34716-34878 + ItemSlot.OverrideLeftClick\n   *  cursorOverride==10 分支 ItemSlot.cs:780-798）：Shift+左键背包格整叠卖出。\n   *  返回是否消费了该次点击——原版该分支恒 return true（:798），卖不掉也不回落\n   *  垃圾桶；调用方（UI.shiftClick）据此短路 */\n  npcShopSell(slot: number): boolean {\n    if (!this.npcShopOpen) return false;          // GetSellOrTrash :187-190 店外走垃圾桶\n    const inv = this.player.inv;\n    const it = inv.slots[slot];\n    if (!it) return true;                          // 空格无动作（IsAir 早退 ItemSlot.cs:535）\n    if (it.favorited) return true;                 // 收藏禁 Shift 转移（ItemSlot.cs:566）\n    const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(it.id);\n    if (vid < 0) return true;\n    if (!canSellFromShopUi(vid)) return true;      // 钱币 71-74 → null 不动作（:191-194）\n    const memoKey = `${vid}:${it.prefix ?? 0}`;\n    const quote = computeSellValue(vid, it.stack, {\n      priceAdjustment: this.shopHappinessMul,       // currentShoppingSettings.PriceAdjustment\n      discountAvailable: this.player.equipStats.discount, // :34917 discountAvailable\n      sellbackAmount: this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0, // :34739 GetAmount\n    });\n    if (!quote.sellable) {\n      // SellItem false 且 value==0 → 白送入店（ItemSlot.cs:790-795 AddItemToShop + 音效 7）\n      this.consumeSellbackMemo(memoKey, it.stack);  // Chest.AddItemToShop :653 Remove\n      inv.slots[slot] = null;                       // item.TurnToAir()（:794）\n      this.sfx.play('pickup');                      // SoundID 7 = Grab（:795）\n      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n      if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();\n      return true;\n    }\n    const before = this.coinsOwned();\n    inv.slots[slot] = null;                         // 先腾格再入币（:787 TurnToAir 在结算后）\n    this.gainCopper(quote.copper);                  // :34745-34868 铂/金/银/铜换装\n    if (this.coinsOwned() - before !== quote.copper) {\n      // 装不下所有币 → 整体回滚（Player.cs:34727-34731 全仓克隆 + :34869-34876 还原；\n      // 本仓等价实现 = 撤回已入币 + 复原物品格），物品不动、回购记不动\n      const gained = this.coinsOwned() - before;\n      if (gained > 0) this.spendCopper(gained);\n      inv.slots[slot] = it;\n      return true;\n    }\n    this.consumeSellbackMemo(memoKey, it.stack);    // chest.AddItemToShop → Remove（Chest.cs:653）\n    this.sfx.play('coin');                          // SoundID 18 = Coins（:788）\n    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n    if (this.dialogNpc) this.openNpcShop();         // 刷新面板余额（同 npcShopBuy）\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** ItemShopSellbackHelper.Remove（ItemShopSellbackHelper.cs:45-63）：扣减记账，\n   *  余量 <=0 删键 */\n  private consumeSellbackMemo(memoKey: string, stack: number): void {\n    const left = (this.shopSellbackMemo.get(memoKey) ?? 0) - stack;\n    if (left <= 0) this.shopSellbackMemo.delete(memoKey);\n    else this.shopSellbackMemo.set(memoKey, left);\n  }\n"
}
